  sum normalized to 1. For central, S0=1.

ParamObj:
```
class ParamObj:
    def __init__(self,n,base_log,feats):
       self.n=n; self.base=base_log; self.feats=feats
    def __call__(self,v):
       y=np.einsum('i,ij->j', v, self.feats)
       a=np.exp(self.base + y)
       a=np.clip(a,0,None)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e300
       a=a/s
       # conv score
       n=self.n
       L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
       h=np.clip(h,0,None)
       M=h.max()
       return float(2*n*M)
```
But L can be precomputed in obj to avoid bit each call. Store self.L. Also could reuse? no.

Greedy heuristic:
```
def greedy_sequence(n, rng, max_steps=2000, frac_min=0.2, frac_max=1.0):
   a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   for step in range(max_steps):
       i=rng.integers(0,n)
       # compute delta
       aff=b[i:i+n]  # copy? avoid modifying before? We'll use view.
       coeff=a[0:n]  # view
       # linear
       LIN=np.empty(n); mask=coeff>1e-300
       LIN[~mask]=np.inf
       num=1-aff # positive because feasible
       # due numerical aff<=1+eps; clip
       num=np.maximum(num,0)
       LIN[mask]=num[mask]/coeff[mask]
       delta=np.min(LIN)
       # quadratic for k=2i
       if 2*i < len(b):
           x=a[i]
           slack=1-b[2*i]
           if slack >= -1e-9:
              # solve δ^2 + 2xδ + ( -slack) <=0? old b +2xδ+δ^2 <=1 => δ^2+2xδ-(1-b)<=0
              # positive root = -x + sqrt(x^2 + slack)
              rad=x*x + max(slack,0)
              qd=np.sqrt(rad)-x
              if qd < delta: delta=qd
       if not finite or delta<=0: continue
       frac=rng.uniform(frac_min,frac_max)
       # also cap delta maybe 10 to avoid too large? under constraints okay
       d=frac*delta
       if d<=0: continue
       old=a[i]
       # update b
       # add vector length n: 2*d*coeff; if quad add d^2
       inc=2*d*coeff
       if 2*i < len(b): inc[i] += d*d  # coeff at j=i is old? add d^2. Note if i located within 0..n-1 yes.
       b[i:i+n] += inc
       a[i] = old + d
       # due numerical, ensure b<=1+eps? Could clip b to1? But then constraints broken. We can if b>1 set d back? For heuristic, clip b to 1 and maybe reduce? Simpler: after update if max(b)>1+1e-7: revert? We computed delta min, numerical okay. But if using view aff earlier, b updated might exceed due using same b? fine.
   S=a.sum()
   if S<0.01: return None
   # scale to max=1? score invariant; normalize sum=1 for consistency. a /= S. But if max>1 due numerical, rescale a /= maxB? We can scale to sum1 anyway.
   a /= S
   return a
```

High-dim gradient function in detail:
```
def run_gradient(a_init, n, rng, time_limit, start_lr=0.15, p_sequence=None, verbose=True):
   a=np.array(a_init,dtype=float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum(); if S<=0: return None
   a=a/S
   L=...
   b,M=conv_max(a,L)
   current=2*n*M
   best=a.copy(); best_score=current
   lr=start_lr
   stall=0
   iter=0
   phases maybe list
   total_iter_est? Use time.
   p = p_sequence[min(len,iter//phase_len)]
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # choose p based on iter fraction if p_sequence None
       ...
       # weights q
       s=b/M
       w=s**(p-1)  # if p=1, w=1
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw<=0 or not finite: q=np.full(2n-1,1/(2n-1)) else q=w/sw
       # T cross
       fa=rfft(a,L); fq=rfft(q[::-1],L)
       h=irfft(fa*fq)
       T=h[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
       T=np.clip(T,0,None)
       G = a * (T/M - a)
       # To avoid all negative maybe subtract weighted mean
       meanG=np.dot(a,G)
       D=G-meanG
       dmax=np.max(np.abs(D))
       if dmax <=1e-15: break
       d=D/dmax
       # Optional momentum: store prev d? skip
       factor=lr
       accepted=False
       for attempts in range(3):
           a2=a*np.exp(-factor*d)
           a2=np.clip(a2,1e-300,1000)
           s2=a2.sum(); a2=a2/s2
           b2,M2=conv_max(a2,L)
           score2=2*n*M2
           if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < current + (0.005*current if iter<2000 else 0.0):
                accept; break
           factor*=0.5
       if not accept:
           lr*=0.7
           if lr<1e-4: break
           stall +=1
           if stall>100:
              # perturb
              a=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,a.size)); a/=a.sum(); b,M=conv_max(a,L); current=2*n*M; lr=0.15; stall=0
       else:
           a=a2; b=b2; M=M2; current=score2
           if current < best_score:
              best_score=current; best=a.copy(); stall=0
           else:
              if iter % 50==0 and current<best_score+0.05: pass
           iter+=1
           # adapt lr: if improved a lot, increase slightly? if current<best_score*0.999: lr=min(0.4,lr*1.05)
       if iter % 500==0: print progress
   return best,best_score
```

We just accept if score2 <= current*(1+temp). If not, reduce factor; maybe accept reduced if still worsening within temp. This may allow escapes. However if all factors worsen, we revert and reduce lr.
After reducing many, we can perturb.

def _is_power_of_two? no.
def next_power_two(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For m=2n-1.

```
def convolve_fft_max(a, L):
    n=a.size
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.clip(c,0.0,1.0e300)
    return c, float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
```
`clip 1e300` maybe no.

```
def cross_corr_qa(q,a,L):
    n=a.size
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fq=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1],L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fq)
    if n==1:
       return np.array([q[0]*a[0]])? T_0=a0 q0.
    T=h[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
    T=np.clip(T,0.0,1e300)
    return T
```
For n=1, T[0]=a0 q0. But run_gradient not for n<8.

Parameter optimize with class:
```
class ParamCallable:
    def __init__(self,n,L,base,feats):
       self.n=n; self.L=L; self.base=base; self.feats=feats
    def __call__(self,v):
       raw=np.einsum('i,ij->j', v, self.feats)
       y=4.0*np.tanh(raw/4.0)
       a=np.exp(self.base+y)
       a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: return 1e100
       a=a/s
       b,M=convolve_fft_max(a,self.L)
       return float(2.0*self.n*M)
```

def optimize_params(n, base, feats, rng, time_limit, K=12):
    best=None; best_score=1e100
    L=...
    if minimize is None: return
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*K
    starts=[np.zeros(K)]
    for _ in range(3): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.3,K))
    # linear trend start: v[0]=0.5? feature t maybe. Add [0.5,0,-?].
    for start in starts:
       if time > limit: break
       try:
          res=minimize(ParamCallable(n,L,base,feats), start, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':500,'ftol':1e-12,'maxls':20})
          if res is not None and finite:
             a=param_to_seq(base,feats,np.asarray(res.x)); s=score_seq(a); update
          # also evaluate start
       except: pass
```

def greedy_sequence(n, rng, max_steps=1200):
   a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   for _ in range(max_steps):
      i=int(rng.integers(0,n))
      aff=b[i:i+n]
      coeff=a
      slack=np.maximum(1.0 - aff, 0.0) # length n view? returns new
      lin=np.full(n, np.inf)
      mask=coeff > 1e-300
      if np.any(mask):
         lin[mask]=slack[mask]/(2.0*coeff[mask])
      delta=float(np.min(lin))
      x=a[i]
      ki=2*i
      if ki < b.size:
         sl=1.0 - b[ki]
         if sl >= -1e-12:
             rad=x*x + max(sl,0.0)
             qd=np.sqrt(rad) - x
             if qd < delta: delta=float(qd)
      if not math.isfinite(delta) or delta <= 1e-15: continue
      frac=float(rng.uniform(0.35,1.0))
      d=delta*frac
      # avoid negative due roots? d positive
      old=x
      inc=2.0*d*coeff
      if ki < b.size:
           inc[i] += d*d
      b[i:i+n] += inc
      a[i] = old + d
   S=a.sum()
   if S <= 1e-9: return None
   a=a/S
   return a
```
But `coeff=a` is full length n, good. `inc=2*d*coeff` full length n; b update uses full length n values a_j for k=i+j. That's correct. However for j such that i+j>=2n? i max n-1, j max n-1 =>2n-2 ok.
Potential issue: `coeff` includes a[i] (old x). If x updated? We use old x in inc; correct.
After many steps, b max may exceed due numerical; we clip slack but not b. If b[k] becomes 1+1e-9, slack negative clipped to0; capacities okay. At end score uses max. Feasible enough.
Performance: `lin=np.full(n,inf)` each step allocation; could preallocate but okay. Use `np.empty` and fill inf? fine.

```python
"""
Search for low-score non-negative sequences for

    score = 2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)^2).

The program combines:
  1. a central-binomial seed (asymptotically about pi/2),
  2. low-dimensional log-polynomial parameter optimization,
  3. a greedy feasible-packing heuristic,
  4. a smoothed-power-norm projected gradient search in log-space,
  5. random block/local search polishing.

All sequences are normalized to sum 1 internally, because the score is scale
invariant.  The final sequence returned is the best one found under the exact
evaluator.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None


def _normalize_positive(arr):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a[a < 1e-300] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _next_power_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int(m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_convolution_max(a, L):
    """Full self-convolution via FFT, clipped tiny negative artifacts."""
    n = int(a.size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, 1e300)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c, 0.0
    return c, float(c.max())


def _score_normalized(a):
    """Score for a sum-normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    L = _next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    _, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _cross_correlation_q_a(q, a, L):
    """
    T_i = sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j * q_{i+j}, for i=0..n-1.
    q has length 2n-1.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(q[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fq, L)
    # Valid indices: t = 2n-2-i for i=0..n-1.
    T = h[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]
    T = np.where(np.isfinite(T), T, 0.0)
    T = np.clip(T, 0.0, 1e300)
    return T


def _make_central(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n, normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return _normalize_positive(c)


def _make_features(n, k):
    """Bounded, normalized feature matrix for low-dimensional log-shape search."""
    if n <= 1:
        feats = np.zeros((k, 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return feats

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = t - 0.5

    cols = [
        u,
        u * u,
        u * u * u,
        t * t - t,
        t * t * t - t * t,
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t) - 1.0,
        np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t),
        np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t) - 1.0,
        np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * t),
        np.log1p(10.0 * t),
        np.log1p(10.0 * (1.0 - t)),
    ]

    while len(cols) < k:
        cols.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))

    feats = np.vstack(cols[:k]).astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    for j in range(k):
        col = feats[j]
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(col)))
        if mx > 1e-15:
            feats[j] = col / mx
    return feats


class _ParamCallable:
    """Callable for scipy minimize; no closures."""

    def __init__(self, n, L, base_log, feats):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.L = int(L)
        self.base_log = np.asarray(base_log, dtype=np.float64)
        self.feats = np.asarray(feats, dtype=np.float64)

    def __call__(self, v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        raw = np.einsum("i,ij->j", v, self.feats)
        y = 3.0 * np.tanh(raw / 3.0)
        a = np.exp(self.base_log + y)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            return 1e100
        a = a / s
        _, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, self.L)
        if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
            return 1e100
        return float(2.0 * self.n * m)


def _param_to_sequence(base_log, feats, v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    raw = np.einsum("i,ij->j", v, feats)
    y = 3.0 * np.tanh(raw / 3.0)
    a = np.exp(base_log + y)
    return _normalize_positive(a)


def _optimize_parameters(n, base_log, feats, rng, time_limit_s):
    """Low-dimensional log-polynomial optimization around central seed."""
    if scipy_minimize is None or time_limit_s <= 5.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    L = _next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    k = int(feats.shape[0])
    bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * k

    starts = [np.zeros(k, dtype=np.float64)]
    starts.append(np.concatenate(([0.7, 0.0, 0.0]), np.zeros(max(0, k - 3))))
    starts.append(np.concatenate(([-0.7, 0.0, 0.0]), np.zeros(max(0, k - 3))))
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(0.25 * rng.standard_normal(k))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit_s)

    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        st = _normalize_positive(clipval := st)
        if st is None:
            stz = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            # Parameter vector need not be normalized; only clip finite.
            stz = np.clip(np.asarray(st, dtype=np.float64), -1.0, 1.0)

        seq = _param_to_sequence(base_log, feats, stz)
        if seq is not None:
            sc = _score_normalized(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq

        func = _ParamCallable(n, L, base_log, feats)
        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                func,
                np.clip(stz, -1.0, 1.0).copy(),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": 180, "ftol": 1e-11, "maxls": 20},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if x.size == k and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                seq = _param_to_sequence(base_log, feats, np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0))
                if seq is not None:
                    sc = _score_normalized(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_seq = seq

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

    return best_seq, best_score


def _greedy_sequence(n, rng, max_steps):
    """
    Greedy construction of a sequence with convolution mostly <= 1.
    Used mainly as a diversified starting point.
    """
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    nsteps = int(max(max_steps, 1))

    for _ in range(nsteps):
        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        aff = b[i:i + n]
        coeff = a
        slack = np.maximum(1.0 - aff, 0.0)

        lin = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = coeff > 1e-300
        if np.any(mask):
            lin[mask] = slack[mask] / (2.0 * coeff[mask])
        delta = float(np.min(lin))

        ki = 2 * i
        x = float(a[i])
        if ki < b.size:
            sl = float(1.0 - b[ki])
            if sl >= -1e-12:
                rad = x * x + max(sl, 0.0)
                qd = float(np.sqrt(rad) - x)
                if qd < delta:
                    delta = qd

        if not np.isfinite(delta) or delta <= 1e-15:
            continue

        frac = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.0))
        d = frac * delta
        if d <= 0.0:
            continue

        old = x
        inc = 2.0 * d * coeff
        if ki < b.size:
            inc[i] += d * d
        b[i:i + n] += inc
        a[i] = old + d

    return _normalize_positive(a)


def _random_perturb_best(best, current, rng, n):
    """Return a perturbed version of best (or current) for restarts."""
    base = best if best is not None else current
    if base is None:
        return None

    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    base[base < 1e-300] = 0.0
    s = base.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    base /= s

    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.45:
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
        cand = base * np.exp(noise)
    elif r < 0.85:
        block = int(rng.integers(max(8, n // 500), max(16, n // 50)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - block)))
        cand = base.copy()
        step = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.22)))
        cand[start:start + block] *= step
        if rng.random() < 0.3:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
    else:
        # mirror-ish perturbation
        cand = 0.5 * (base + base[::-1])
        cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n))

    return _normalize_positive(cand)


def _gradient_run(a_init, n, rng, time_limit_s):
    """
    Smoothed power-norm projected multiplicative gradient descent.
    The exact evaluator remains authoritative, but this loop uses fast FFT.
    """
    a = _normalize_positive(a_init)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    L = _next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return None, float("inf")

    current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit_s)
    lr = 0.18
    it = 0
    reject_streak = 0
    improve_streak = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    q_len = 2 * n - 1

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = 3.0 + 27.0 * min(1.0, float(it) / 3500.0)

        s = b / m
        if p <= 2.05:
            q = np.full(q_len, 1.0 / q_len, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = s ** (p - 1.0)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
                q = np.full(q_len, 1.0 / q_len, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                q = w / sw

        T = _cross_correlation_q_a(q, a, L)
        G = a * (T / m - a)
        meanG = float(np.dot(a, G))
        D = G - meanG
        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))

        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-16:
            cand = _random_perturb_best(best, a, rng, n)
            if cand is None:
                break
            a = cand
            b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
            if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
                break
            current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
            lr = 0.22
            reject_streak = 0
            continue

        d = D / dmax
        accepted = False
        factor = lr

        for _ in range(3):
            a2 = a * np.exp(-factor * d)
            a2 = np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0.0)
            a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s2 = float(a2.sum())
            if s2 <= 1e-12:
                factor *= 0.5
                continue
            a2 = a2 / s2

            b2, m2 = _fft_convolution_max(a2, L)
            score2 = float(2.0 * n * m2)
            if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < current_score - 1e-13:
                a = a2
                b = b2
                m = m2
                current_score = score2
                accepted = True
                break
            factor *= 0.45
            if factor < 1e-7:
                break

        if accepted:
            it += 1
            reject_streak = 0
            if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = current_score
                improve_streak = 0
                lr = min(0.45, lr * 1.03)
            else:
                improve_streak += 1
                if improve_streak > 1200:
                    cand = _random_perturb_best(best, a, rng, n)
                    if cand is not None:
                        a = cand
                        b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
                        current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
                        lr = 0.25
                        improve_streak = 0
        else:
            reject_streak += 1
            lr *= 0.75
            if lr < 1e-5 or reject_streak > 55:
                cand = _random_perturb_best(best, a, rng, n)
                if cand is None:
                    break
                a = cand
                b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
                if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
                    break
                current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
                lr = 0.24
                reject_streak = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 60.0:
            print(
                f"[gradient] t={time.monotonic():.1f} "
                f"n={n} it={it} current={current_score:.8f} "
                f"best={best_score:.8f}"
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def _local_block_search(a_init, n, rng, time_limit_s):
    """Random multiplicative block perturbations, accepting only improvements."""
    a = _normalize_positive(a_init)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    L = _next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
    current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit_s)

    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        block = int(rng.integers(max(8, n // 400), max(16, n // 25)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - block)))

        cand = a.copy()
        mult = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, 1.0)))
        cand[start:start + block] *= mult
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = _normalize_positive(cand)
        if cand is None:
            it += 1
            continue

        b2, m2 = _fft_convolution_max(cand, L)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * m2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            a = cand
            b = b2
            m = m2
            current_score = sc
        elif sc < current_score + 1e-12:
            # small local random walk around best/current
            if sc < current_score:
                a = cand
                b = b2
                m = m2
                current_score = sc
            else:
                r = time.monotonic()
                # occasional annealing for the first part of the search
                if current_score - best_score > 1e-8 and r % 17 < 1:
                    a = cand
                    b = b2
                    m = m2
                    current_score = sc
        else:
            # occasional reset to best if drifting too far
            if current_score > best_score * 1.08:
                a = best.copy()
                b, m = _fft_convolution_max(a, L)
                current_score = float(2.0 * n * m)

        it += 1
        if it % 120 == 0 and time.monotonic() - last_print > 50.0:
            print(
                f"[local] t={time.monotonic():.1f} "
                f"n={n} it={it} current={current_score:.8f} "
                f"best={best_score:.8f}"
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0, seed=20240714):
    """
    Return the best non-negative coefficient sequence found within budget_s.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(seed) else 20240714)
    total_budget = float(max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - 6.0)

    # Select a working length.  If the provided parent has a reasonable length,
    # use it; otherwise use a power-friendly length.
    parent = None
    N = 4096
    try:
        raw_parent = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        parr = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=np.float64)
        if parr.ndim == 1 and parr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(parr)):
            pnorm = _normalize_positive(np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1000.0))
            if pnorm is not None:
                parent = pnorm
                if 1024 <= parr.size <= 10000:
                    N = int(parr.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    N = int(N)
    if N < 256:
        N = 256
    if N > 10000:
        N = 8192

    print(f"[init] using n={N}")

    central = _make_central(N)
    central_score = _score_normalized(central)
    best_seq = central.copy()
    best_score = central_score
    print(f"[init] central score={best_score:.10f}")

    candidates = [central.copy()]

    # Parent, if compatible length.
    if parent is not None and parent.size == N:
        ps = _score_normalized(parent)
        if np.isfinite(ps):
            candidates.append(parent.copy())
            print(f"[init] parent score={ps:.10f}")

    # Low-dimensional parameter search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        base_log = np.log(np.maximum(central, 1e-300))
        feats = _make_features(N, 12)
        ptime = min(160.0, max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 80.0))
        print("[phase] parameter search")
        pseq, psc = _optimize_parameters(N, base_log, feats, rng, ptime)
        if pseq is not None and np.isfinite(psc):
            if psc < best_score:
                best_seq = pseq
                best_score = psc
                print(f"[param] improved best to {best_score:.10f}")
            candidates.append(pseq.copy())
            # reversed version often explores a different basin
            pr = _normalize_positive(pseq[::-1])
            if pr is not None:
                candidates.append(pr.copy())
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, total_budget - 6.0)

    # Greedy diversification.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        print("[phase] greedy seeds")
        gtime = min(70.0, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0))
        per_seed = max(350, int(gtime * 1000.0 / max(1.0, float(N))))
        per_seed = min(2500, per_seed)
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            g = _greedy_sequence(N, rng, per_seed)
            if g is not None:
                gs = _score_normalized(g)
                if np.isfinite(gs):
                    if gs < best_score:
                        best_seq = g.copy()
                        best_score = gs
                        print(f"[greedy] improved best to {best_score:.10f}")
                    candidates.append(g.copy())

    # Remove None candidates, duplicates, and sort by fast exact score.
    cleaned = []
    seen_norm = []
    for c in candidates:
        cc = _normalize_positive(c)
        if cc is None or cc.size != N:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(cc)):
            continue
        norm = np.sum(np.abs(cc))
        is_dup = False
        for sn in seen_norm:
            if np.min(np.abs(coshval := np.mean(2.0 * cc * sn) - 1.0)) < 0.005:
                is_dup = True
                break
        if not is_dup:
            seen_norm.append(cc.copy())
            cleaned.append(cc)

    scored = []
    for cc in cleaned:
        sc = _score_normalized(cc)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, cc))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    if not scored:
        scored = [(central_score, central.copy())]

    # Run gradient search on the top few candidates.
    weights = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]
    remaining_initial = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0)

    idx = 0
    for sc0, cseq in scored[:3]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        alloc = remaining_initial * weights[min(idx, len(weights) - 1)]
        if alloc < 15.0:
            break
        print(
            f"[phase] gradient start score={sc0:.10f} "
            f"alloc={alloc:.1f}s"
        )
        gseq, gsc = _gradient_run(cseq, N, rng, alloc)
        if gseq is not None and np.isfinite(gsc):
            if gsc < best_score:
                best_seq = gseq.copy()
                best_score = gsc
                print(f"[gradient] improved best to {best_score:.10f}")
            candidates.append(gseq.copy())
            candidates.append(_normalize_positive(gseq[::-1]))
        idx += 1

    # Final local block polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        lalloc = min(180.0, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 4.0))
        print(f"[phase] local block polish alloc={lalloc:.1f}s")
        lseq, lsc = _local_block_search(best_seq, N, rng, lalloc)
        if lseq is not None and np.isfinite(lsc):
            if lsc < best_score:
                best_seq = lseq.copy()
                best_score = lsc
                print(f"[local] improved best to {best_score:.10f}")

    # Verify a small set of candidates with the authoritative evaluator.
    exact_seq = best_seq
    exact_score = float("inf")

    verification = [best_seq, central]
    if parent is not None and parent.size == N:
        verification.append(parent)
    for _, c in scored[:4]:
        verification.append(c)

    for seq in verification:
        if seq is None:
            continue
        arr = _normalize_positive(seq)
        if arr is None or arr.size != N:
            continue
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            val = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < exact_score:
            exact_score = float(val)
            exact_seq = arr.copy()

    if exact_seq is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(exact_seq)):
        exact_seq = central.copy()

    final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(exact_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)]
    try:
        final_val = evaluate_sequence(final_list)  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        final_val = float("inf")

    if np.isfinite(final_val):
        print(f"[final] exact score={final_val:.12f} length={len(final_list)}")
    else:
        print(f"[final] evaluator returned non-finite; returning safe sequence length={len(final_list)}")

    return final_list
```
